=== open-node.ts CONTRACT ===

Pure domain module. NO vscode import. NO node:fs import. NO side effects.

Imports:
- isNodeId from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' (use same @ts-expect-error ESM pattern as roots.ts:3-4)
- type NodeId from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with { 'resolution-mode': 'import' }
- type URI from '@foam/core'
- type PrivacyPolicy, DenyReason from './privacy'

Export a discriminated union result type:

```typescript
export type OpenNodeDeny =
  | { allowed: false; reason: 'invalid_id' }
  | { allowed: false; reason: 'unknown_id'; nodeId: NodeId }
  | { allowed: false; reason: 'stale_mapping'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI }
  | { allowed: false; reason: 'privacy_denied'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI; denyReason: DenyReason };

export type OpenNodeAllow = { allowed: true; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI };

export type OpenNodeResult = OpenNodeAllow | OpenNodeDeny;
```

Export the main function:

```typescript
export function resolveNodeOpen(
  payload: unknown,
  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI>,
  policy: PrivacyPolicy,
  isInWorkspace: (uri: URI) => boolean
): OpenNodeResult
```

Validation order (EXACT, each check runs only if prior checks pass):
1. `!isNodeId(payload)` => `{ allowed: false, reason: 'invalid_id' }`
2. `idToUri.get(payload)` miss => `{ allowed: false, reason: 'unknown_id', nodeId: payload }`
3. `!isInWorkspace(uri)` => `{ allowed: false, reason: 'stale_mapping', nodeId: payload, uri }`
4. `uri.scheme !== 'file'` OR `!policy.decide(uri.toFsPath()).allowed` => privacy_denied with the inner DenyReason from the decision (for non-file scheme use 'outside_roots' as denyReason)
5. else `{ allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri }`

Step 4 calls policy.decide() fresh every time — never cache.

=== test_open_node.test.ts CONTRACT ===

All test names prefixed `test_open_node_`. Use vitest (describe/it/expect).
Use createPrivacyPolicy from '../privacy' with fs.mkdtempSync temp roots (same pattern as test_privacy_policy.test.ts). Never touch real HOME or real profile.md.

Required tests (minimum 6, add more if useful):
1. test_open_node_invalid_id — non-string, empty string, "not:a:valid:id", number => invalid_id
2. test_open_node_unknown_id — valid NodeId format but not in map => unknown_id
3. test_open_node_stale_mapping — in map but isInWorkspace returns false => stale_mapping
4. test_open_node_privacy_denied_mandatory — in map, in workspace, but URI points to mandatory exclusion (knowledge/me/profile.md sentinel) => privacy_denied with denyReason='mandatory_exclusion'
5. test_open_node_privacy_denied_nonfile — in map, in workspace, but URI has scheme='placeholder' => privacy_denied with denyReason='outside_roots'
6. test_open_node_allowed — in map, in workspace, passes privacy => allowed with correct uri

For fixtures:
- Create temp dir with fs.mkdtempSync
- Write a sentinel file `knowledge/me/profile.md` in the temp root (this is the mandatory exclusion)
- Write a normal allowed file (e.g. `notes/hello.md`)
- Create policy with createPrivacyPolicy({ roots: [tempRoot], excludeGlobs: [] })
- Build idToUri map manually with makeNodeId from roots.ts or protocol
- isInWorkspace callback: simple Set lookup or always-true/always-false lambdas

Cleanup: afterAll or afterEach removes temp dir.

Do NOT test privacy internals (inode, lookalike, glob matching) — already covered by test_privacy_policy.
Do NOT import vscode anywhere in the test file.

# Lazarus Codex

Turn rough intent into bounded, verified work while keeping main context focused.

## Load order

1. Read `../../assets/lazarus/adapters/codex-runtime-pilot.md`. This is the Codex activation and optional CLI-review contract; it supersedes historical PAL-retirement and Claude-only routing text for this host's explicitly authorized pilot.
2. State the objective, owned artifacts, and observable checks. Use the compact Plan, Do, Check, Act loop in that adapter for ordinary work.
3. Load `../../assets/lazarus/workflow.md` and its selected detailed route only when the task needs the full scaffold. Do not load every registry for a direct answer or a small scoped edit.
4. For durable PDCA cycles, load `../../assets/lazarus/skills/pdca-cycle-advisor.md` with `pdca-loop.md`; resolve the helper as this plugin root's `scripts/pdca_cycle.py`.

- Main owns interpretation, routing, PDCA gates, synthesis, final response, and prompt-system self-modification. Delegate substantial code/build work to `gpt-5.3-codex-spark`/`xhigh` when a separate worker improves scale or verification; keep small scoped work in-house.
- Use Bunya first when ALIVE for eligible bulk verifiable extraction, text and repo shards, following `bunya-session.md`; user preference for native non-code/prose/extraction/default fallback is `gpt-5.4-mini`/`xhigh`. Discover actual host model availability before dispatch; report unavailable routes instead of silently substituting.

- GPT-6 boundary advisor calls use `gpt-6-astra` with reasoning `high`: send <=500 word packets, require <=200 word responses, and use durable `pdca_cycle.py` claims to prevent duplicate consults; advice never proves acceptance.

# Codex runtime and optional PAL pilot

Owner: Codex host adapter. Authorized 2026-09-14. This host-specific pilot supersedes historical "never route PAL" statements only for the separate `lazarus-pal-pilot` server. The legacy embedded PAL bundle remains retired; do not restore old API keys, retired shims, or old permissive CLI presets.

## Activation and work loop

Invoke the installed `lazarus` skill or ask "Use Lazarus". If the skill is absent, inspect plugin enabled state before guessing another invocation. A plugin reinstall requires a fresh thread to expose updated skills and tools.

1. Plan: state objective, scoped artifacts, assumptions, observable checks, and rollback for operational changes. Plan-only requests remain plan-only.
2. Do: execute authorized work. Main handles small scoped work and prompt-system self-modification. Delegate substantial implementation when it materially helps, with disjoint ownership and concrete checks.
3. Check: inspect actual artifacts and run proportionate checks. Advice and worker self-report never prove acceptance.
4. Act: integrate verified results, record material failures, and report remaining limits. Existing hooks remain active; do not bypass a rejection.

No mandatory external model review for every task. Do not load unrelated scaffold registries or start a second review only to satisfy an obsolete Claude-only sequence. Preserve durable cycle tracking when explicitly requested or already active.

## Optional CLI review

The independently registered `lazarus-pal-pilot` MCP server exposes `review_plan`; discover its exact callable name in the current host before using it. It uses unmodified PAL 9.8.2 CLI execution through a small local entrypoint, not PAL's full API-provider server.

- `provider="agy"`: Gemini `gemini-3.8-flash-high`, effort high, mode plan, sandbox. Review only. Never combine plan mode with `--disable-slash-commands`, which disables the mode.
- `provider="codex"`: GPT-5.5/high advisory review in a read-only sandbox. `account="work"` or `"revolut"` selects an existing separately authenticated CLI home. This review role does not change the Spark implementation preference.
- Pass a self-contained plan, explicit working directory when needed, and a narrow review question. Default working directory is `/tmp`.
- Timeout, nonzero exit, invalid output, or quota exhaustion means failed/degraded review. Checkpoint; do not replay edits or automatically rotate accounts. Retry only with an explicit reason and bounded scope.
- PAL and AGY responses are advisory. Host retains final verification and workflow ownership.

CLI fallback, when the MCP tool is not yet exposed: pipe the supplied plan into `lazarus-review --provider agy`, or `lazarus-review --provider codex --account revolut`. Credentials remain managed by the CLIs.

## Saved Codex accounts

Use `lazarus-account work` or `lazarus-account revolut` to check login status. Append normal Codex arguments to launch that account, e.g. `lazarus-account revolut -C /absolute/project/path`. Each launch selects a child process's existing account home; the main app account is unchanged. Authenticate with `lazarus-account revolut login` only if status or a live request requires it. Never copy credential files, log out the other account, or infer current quota from stale logs. This is explicit CLI selection, not desktop account switching or automatic quota pooling.

The pilot project's README, tests, and evidence are no longer present on this host; use the live launcher `/Users/slee65/.local/bin/lazarus-account`. This adapter is local to this Mac; do not export its absolute launcher paths as universal defaults.

User labels: `work` selects the current `~/.codex` login; `revolut` selects the existing `~/.codex-b` login. `primary` and `secondary` remain compatibility aliases.

# Adapter: Software

Load this adapter only when the user explicitly asks for code, debugging, implementation, tests, APIs, data pipelines, executable notebooks, build output, or changes inside a codebase.

## Vocabulary Mapping

Software tasks may use these terms because they match the domain:

| Software term | Generic equivalent |
|---|---|
| Object or class | Artifact with state and behavior. |
| Interface | Handoff contract or public boundary. |
| Test | Deterministic acceptance check. |
| Refactor | Improve structure while preserving behavior. |
| Module | Artifact group with one responsibility. |
| Service | Component that owns one capability. |

## Procedure

1. Read the relevant files before editing.
2. Identify the smallest behavior-preserving or feature-complete change.
3. Prefer existing project patterns over new abstractions.
4. Add or update tests when the change affects behavior.
5. Run the narrowest useful validation first.
6. Report changed paths and validation results.

## Checks

- The change is scoped to the requested behavior.
- Existing behavior is preserved unless the user requested otherwise.
- Tests or equivalent checks were run, or the reason they could not run is reported.
- No unrelated files were reformatted or rewritten.

import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
import { relativeTo, isWithinPath } from '@foam/core';
// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
import { makeNodeId, parseNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
import type { NodeId, RootGroup, RootKind } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
  'resolution-mode': 'import',
};

export interface ClassifiedRoot {
  uri: URI;
  kind: RootKind;
}

export interface RootClassification {
  roots: ClassifiedRoot[];
  rootGroups: RootGroup[];
}

const ROOT_GROUPS: Record<RootKind, RootGroup> = {
  vault: { root: 'vault', label: 'Vault', color: '#4a9eff' },
  sources: { root: 'sources', label: 'Sources', color: '#ff9f43' },
};

export function classifyRoots(
  workspaceRoots: URI[],
  classification?: Map<URI, RootKind>
): RootClassification {
  const roots = workspaceRoots.map((uri, index): ClassifiedRoot => {
    const mappedKind =
      classification?.get(uri) ??
      [...(classification?.entries() ?? [])].find(([classifiedUri]) =>
        classifiedUri.isEqual(uri)
      )?.[1];
    return {
      uri,
      kind: mappedKind ?? (index === 0 ? 'vault' : 'sources'),
    };
  });

  const rootGroups = Array.from(new Set(roots.map(root => root.kind))).map(
    kind => ({ ...ROOT_GROUPS[kind] })
  );

  return { roots, rootGroups };
}

export function resolveRoot(
  uri: URI,
  classifiedRoots: ClassifiedRoot[]
): ClassifiedRoot | undefined {
  return classifiedRoots.find(root => isWithinPath(uri, root.uri));
}

function isValidNodePath(path: string): boolean {
  return parseNodeId(`vault:${path}`)?.path === path;
}

export function makeResourceNodeId(
  uri: URI,
  classifiedRoots: ClassifiedRoot[]
): NodeId | undefined {
  const root = resolveRoot(uri, classifiedRoots);
  if (!root) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const relativePath = relativeTo(uri.path, root.uri.path);
  if (!isValidNodePath(relativePath)) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return makeNodeId(root.kind, relativePath);
}

  type AccessDecision,

let paperPath: string;
let secretPath: string;
let profileLinkPath: string;
let outsideLinkPath: string;
let sourcesLinkPath: string;

function policy(
  roots: readonly string[] = [vaultRoot, sourcesRoot],

  decision: AccessDecision,
  reason: AccessDecision['reason']
): void {
  expect(decision.reason).toBe(reason);

    );
  });

    expectReason(policy().decide(profileLinkPath), 'mandatory_exclusion');
  });

  it('test_privacy_policy_denies_hardlink_to_profile', () => {
    const hardLinkPath = path.join(vaultRoot, 'notes', 'hard.md');
    fs.linkSync(profilePath, hardLinkPath);
    expectReason(policy().decide(hardLinkPath), 'mandatory_exclusion');
    expectReason(policy().decide(notePath), 'allowed');
  });

  it('test_privacy_policy_denies_symlink_escaping_root', () => {
    expectReason(policy().decide(outsideLinkPath), 'outside_roots');
  });

  it('test_privacy_policy_cross_root_symlink_resolves_to_target_root', () => {
    const decision = policy().decide(sourcesLinkPath);
    expectReason(decision, 'allowed');
    expect(decision.root).toBe(fs.realpathSync.native(sourcesRoot));
    expect(decision.relativePath).toBe('topic/paper.md');
  });

    );
  });

  it('test_privacy_policy_denies_percent_encoded', () => {
    const first = policy().decide(
      path.join(vaultRoot, 'notes', '%2E%2E', 'knowledge', 'me', 'profile.md')
    );
    const second = policy().decide(
      `${vaultRoot}%2Fknowledge%2Fme%2Fprofile.md`

    expectReason(second, 'encoded_path');

    expect(second.canonicalPath).toBeNull();
  });

  it('test_privacy_policy_denies_lookalikes', () => {
    expectReason(

    );
  });

  it('test_privacy_policy_denies_non_string_and_relative', () => {
    expectReason(policy().decide(undefined), 'invalid_input');
    expectReason(policy().decide(42), 'invalid_input');
    expectReason(policy().decide(''), 'invalid_input');
    expectReason(policy().decide('notes/a.md'), 'relative_path');

      expectReason(lockedDecision, 'mandatory_exclusion');

    } finally {

      excludeGlobs: [],

import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as path from 'node:path';

export const MANDATORY_EXCLUSIONS: readonly string[] = [
  'knowledge/me/profile.md',
];

export interface PrivacyConfig {
  roots: readonly string[];
  excludeGlobs: readonly string[];
}

export type DenyReason =
  | 'invalid_input'
  | 'encoded_path'
  | 'lookalike_path'
  | 'relative_path'
  | 'outside_roots'
  | 'mandatory_exclusion'
  | 'configured_exclusion';

export interface AccessDecision {
  allowed: boolean;
  reason: 'allowed' | DenyReason;
  input: string | null;
  canonicalPath: string | null;
  root: string | null;
  relativePath: string | null;
}

export interface PrivacyPolicy {
  readonly version: string;
  decide(input: unknown): AccessDecision;
  isAllowed(input: unknown): boolean;
  purge<K extends string>(keys: Iterable<K>): K[];
}

interface RootEntry {
  readonly raw: string;
  readonly canonical: string;
}

interface FileIdentity {
  readonly dev: number;
  readonly ino: number;
}

function asPosix(relativePath: string): string {
  return relativePath.split(path.sep).join('/');
}

function isWithin(root: string, candidate: string): boolean {
  if (root === candidate) {
    return true;
  }

  const prefix = root.endsWith(path.sep) ? root : `${root}${path.sep}`;
  return candidate.startsWith(prefix);
}

function relativeTo(root: string, candidate: string): string | null {
  const normalisedRoot = path.normalize(root);
  if (!isWithin(normalisedRoot, candidate)) {
    return null;
  }
  return asPosix(path.relative(normalisedRoot, candidate));
}

function canonicaliseRoots(roots: readonly string[]): RootEntry[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(roots)) {
    return [];
  }

  return roots.flatMap(raw => {
    if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length === 0) {
      return [];
    }

    try {
      const canonical = fs.realpathSync.native(raw);
      return fs.statSync(canonical).isDirectory()
        ? [{ raw, canonical }]
        : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  });
}

function fileIdentity(filePath: string): FileIdentity | null {
  try {
    const stats = fs.statSync(filePath);
    return stats.isFile() ? { dev: stats.dev, ino: stats.ino } : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function resolveCanonicalPath(normalisedPath: string): string | null {
  try {
    if (fs.existsSync(normalisedPath)) {
      return fs.realpathSync.native(normalisedPath);
    }

    let cursor = normalisedPath;
    const remainder: string[] = [];
    while (!fs.existsSync(cursor)) {
      const parent = path.dirname(cursor);
      if (parent === cursor) {
        return null;
      }
      remainder.unshift(path.basename(cursor));
      cursor = parent;
    }

    let canonical = fs.realpathSync.native(cursor);
    for (const part of remainder) {
      canonical = path.join(canonical, part);
    }
    return canonical;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function mandatoryMatch(relativePath: string | null): boolean {
  if (relativePath === null) {
    return false;
  }

  const comparable = relativePath.toLowerCase();
  return MANDATORY_EXCLUSIONS.some(
    exclusion => exclusion.toLowerCase() === comparable
  );
}

function globRegex(glob: string): RegExp {
  let expression = '';
  for (let index = 0; index < glob.length; index += 1) {
    const character = glob[index];
    if (character === '*' && glob[index + 1] === '*') {
      index += 1;
      if (glob[index + 1] === '/') {
        expression += '(?:.*/)?';
        index += 1;
      } else {
        expression += '.*';
      }
    } else if (character === '*') {
      expression += '[^/]*';
    } else if (character === '?') {
      expression += '[^/]';
    } else {
      expression += /[\\^$.*+?()[\]{}|]/.test(character)
        ? `\\${character}`
        : character;
    }
  }
  return new RegExp(`^${expression}$`, 'i');
}

function matchesConfiguredGlob(
  relativePath: string,
  glob: string,
  matcher: RegExp
): boolean {
  const target = glob.includes('/')
    ? relativePath
    : relativePath.slice(relativePath.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);
  return matcher.test(target);
}

function makeDecision(
  input: string | null,
  reason: AccessDecision['reason'],
  canonicalPath: string | null = null,
  root: string | null = null,
  relativePath: string | null = null
): AccessDecision {
  return {
    allowed: reason === 'allowed',
    reason,
    input,
    canonicalPath,
    root,
    relativePath,
  };
}

export function createPrivacyPolicy(config: PrivacyConfig): PrivacyPolicy {
  const roots = canonicaliseRoots(config.roots);
  const configuredGlobs = Array.isArray(config.excludeGlobs)
    ? config.excludeGlobs.filter(glob => typeof glob === 'string')
    : [];
  const configuredMatchers = configuredGlobs.map(glob => ({
    glob,
    matcher: globRegex(glob),
  }));
  const mandatoryReferencePaths = roots.flatMap(entry =>
    MANDATORY_EXCLUSIONS.map(exclusion =>
      path.join(entry.canonical, exclusion)
    )
  );
  const cachedMandatoryReferences = mandatoryReferencePaths.map(fileIdentity);
  const version = `privacy-v1:${JSON.stringify({
    roots: roots.map(entry => entry.canonical),
    exclusions: [...MANDATORY_EXCLUSIONS, ...configuredGlobs],
  })}`;

  function decide(input: unknown): AccessDecision {
    const stringInput = typeof input === 'string' ? input : null;
    if (
      typeof input !== 'string' ||
      input.length === 0 ||
      /[\u0000-\u001F\u007F]/u.test(input)
    ) {
      return makeDecision(stringInput, 'invalid_input');
    }
    if (input.includes('%')) {
      return makeDecision(input, 'encoded_path');
    }
    if (
      input.includes('\\') ||
      /[․․‥…．．／∕⁄]/u.test(input)
    ) {
      return makeDecision(input, 'lookalike_path');
    }
    if (!path.isAbsolute(input)) {
      return makeDecision(input, 'relative_path');
    }

    const normalisedInput = path.normalize(input);
    const canonicalPath = resolveCanonicalPath(normalisedInput);
    if (canonicalPath === null) {
      return makeDecision(input, 'outside_roots');
    }

    const winningRoot = roots
      .filter(entry => isWithin(entry.canonical, canonicalPath))
      .sort((left, right) => right.canonical.length - left.canonical.length)[0];
    if (winningRoot === undefined) {
      return makeDecision(input, 'outside_roots', canonicalPath);
    }

    const relativePath = relativeTo(winningRoot.canonical, canonicalPath);
    if (relativePath === null) {
      return makeDecision(input, 'outside_roots', canonicalPath);
    }

    const inputMatchesMandatory = roots.some(entry =>
      [entry.raw, entry.canonical].some(root =>
        mandatoryMatch(relativeTo(root, normalisedInput))
      )
    );
    if (mandatoryMatch(relativePath) || inputMatchesMandatory) {
      return makeDecision(
        input,
        'mandatory_exclusion',
        canonicalPath,
        winningRoot.canonical,
        relativePath
      );
    }

    const candidateIdentity = fileIdentity(canonicalPath);
    const refreshedMandatoryReferences = mandatoryReferencePaths.map(
      fileIdentity
    );
    if (
      candidateIdentity !== null &&
      [...cachedMandatoryReferences, ...refreshedMandatoryReferences].some(
        reference =>
          reference !== null &&
          reference.dev === candidateIdentity.dev &&
          reference.ino === candidateIdentity.ino
      )
    ) {
      return makeDecision(
        input,
        'mandatory_exclusion',

hook: PostToolUse

/**
 * Shared message types between the extension host and the graph webview.
 * This file must remain free of VS Code and Node.js imports.
 */

export const PA_HUB_PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1 as const;

export const ROOT_KINDS = ['vault', 'sources'] as const;
export type RootKind = (typeof ROOT_KINDS)[number];

export type NodeId = `${RootKind}:${string}`;

export type NodeType =
  | 'note'
  | 'tag'
  | 'placeholder'
  | 'image'
  | 'attachment'
  | string;

export interface NodeInfo {
  id: string;
  type: NodeType;
  title: string;
  properties: { color?: string; [key: string]: unknown };
  tags: Array<{ label: string }>;
  root?: RootKind;
}

export interface GraphData {
  nodeInfo: Record<string, NodeInfo>;
  links: Array<{ source: string; target: string }>;
  rootGroups?: RootGroup[];
  protocolVersion?: number;
}

export interface RootGroup {
  root: RootKind;
  label: string;
  color: string;
}

export interface StyleConfig {
  background?: string;
  fontSize?: number;
  fontFamily?: string;
  lineColor?: string;
  lineWidth?: number;
  particleWidth?: number;
  highlightedForeground?: string;
  node?: {
    note?: string;
    placeholder?: string;
    tag?: string;
    [key: string]: string | undefined;
  };
}

export type GroupMatchProperty = 'type' | 'path' | 'tag' | 'title' | string;

export interface GroupMatch {
  property: GroupMatchProperty;
  value: string;
}

export interface GroupRule {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  color: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  match: GroupMatch;
}

export interface GraphStyle {
  style?: StyleConfig;
  colorMode?: 'none' | 'directory' | 'type';
  groups?: GroupRule[];
  showNodesOfType?: Record<string, boolean>;
}

/** Config for a built-in special type (tag, attachment, image, placeholder) */
export interface BuiltinTypeConfig {
  enabled?: boolean;
  color?: string;
}

/**
 * A named, pre-configured graph view.
 * Also used as raw command args for `pa-hub.show-graph`.
 * Merge order: pa-hub.graph.style → named view → inline config.
 */
export interface GraphViewConfig {
  name?: string;
  colorBy?: 'none' | 'directory' | 'type';
  groups?: GroupRule[];
  /** Visibility and color for built-in types: tag, attachment, image, placeholder */
  show?: Record<string, BuiltinTypeConfig>;
  background?: string;
  fontSize?: number;
  fontFamily?: string;
  lineColor?: string;
}

export interface ShowGraphArgs {
  view?: string;
  config?: GraphViewConfig;
}

export interface FocusRequest {
  requestId: string;
  nodeId: NodeId;
  center?: boolean;
}

export interface FocusResult {
  requestId: string;
  nodeId: NodeId;
  ok: boolean;
  reason?: string;
}

// Extension → Webview
export type ExtensionMessage =
  | { type: 'didUpdateStyle'; payload: GraphStyle }
  | { type: 'didUpdateGraphData'; payload: GraphData }
  | { type: 'didSelectNote'; payload: string }
  | { type: 'didRequestFocus'; payload: FocusRequest }
  | { type: 'didChangeVisibility'; payload: { visible: boolean } };

// Webview → Extension
export type WebviewMessage =
  | { type: 'webviewDidLoad' }
  | { type: 'webviewDidSelectNode'; payload: string }
  | {
      type: 'error';
      payload: {
        message: string;
        filename: string;
        lineno: number;
        colno: number;
        error?: unknown;
      };
    }
  | { type: 'webviewDidBecomeReady'; payload: { protocolVersion: number } }
  | { type: 'webviewDidFocusNode'; payload: FocusResult };

export const EXTENSION_MESSAGE_TYPES = [
  'didUpdateStyle',
  'didUpdateGraphData',
  'didSelectNote',
  'didRequestFocus',
  'didChangeVisibility',
] as const;

export const WEBVIEW_MESSAGE_TYPES = [
  'webviewDidLoad',
  'webviewDidSelectNode',
  'error',
  'webviewDidBecomeReady',
  'webviewDidFocusNode',
] as const;

export type ParseResult<T> =
  | { ok: true; value: T }
  | { ok: false; reason: string };

export interface ParseOptions {
  maxChars?: number;
}

export const MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS = 2_000_000;

function isRootKind(value: unknown): value is RootKind {
  return value === 'vault' || value === 'sources';
}

function isValidNodePath(path: string): boolean {
  if (path.length === 0 || path.length > 1024 || path.startsWith('/')) {
    return false;
  }

  if (path.includes('\\') || path.includes('://')) {
    return false;
  }

  if (/^[A-Za-z]+:/.test(path) || /%2e|%2f|%5c/i.test(path)) {
    return false;
  }

  for (const character of path) {
    const codePoint = character.codePointAt(0);
    if (codePoint !== undefined && (codePoint < 0x20 || codePoint === 0x7f)) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return path
    .split('/')
    .every((segment) => segment !== '' && segment !== '.' && segment !== '..');
}

export function parseNodeId(value: unknown): { root: RootKind; path: string } | null {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 1100) {
    return null;
  }

  const separator = value.indexOf(':');
  if (separator <= 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const root = value.slice(0, separator);
  const path = value.slice(separator + 1);
  if (!isRootKind(root) || !isValidNodePath(path)) {
    return null;
  }

  return { root, path };
}

export function isNodeId(value: unknown): value is NodeId {
  return parseNodeId(value) !== null;
}

export function makeNodeId(root: RootKind, path: string): NodeId {
  const value = `${root}:${path}`;
  if (!isNodeId(value)) {
    throw new RangeError('Invalid node id');
  }
  return value;
}

type MessageRecord = Record<string, unknown>;

interface MessageEnvelope {
  type: string;
  hasPayload: boolean;
  payload: unknown;
}

function isPlainObject(value: unknown): value is MessageRecord {
  return (
    typeof value === 'object' &&
    value !== null &&
    Object.getPrototypeOf(value) === Object.prototype
  );
}

function hasOwn(value: MessageRecord, key: string): boolean {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key);
}

function hasForbiddenKey(value: MessageRecord): boolean {
  return Object.getOwnPropertyNames(value).some(
    (key) => key === '__proto__' || key === 'constructor' || key === 'prototype',
  );
}

function hasExtraKey(value: MessageRecord, allowed: readonly string[]): boolean {
  return Reflect.ownKeys(value).some(
    (key) => typeof key !== 'string' || !allowed.includes(key),
  );
}

function fail(reason: string): ParseResult<never> {
  return { ok: false, reason };
}

function success<T>(value: T): ParseResult<T> {
  return { ok: true, value };
}

function badPayload(field: string): ParseResult<never> {
  return fail(`bad_payload:${field}`);
}

function getMaxChars(options?: ParseOptions): number {
  const maxChars = options?.maxChars;
  return typeof maxChars === 'number' && Number.isFinite(maxChars) && maxChars >= 0
    ? maxChars
    : MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS;
}

function parseEnvelope(
  input: unknown,
  allowedTypes: readonly string[],
  options?: ParseOptions,
): ParseResult<MessageEnvelope> {
  if (!isPlainObject(input)) {
    return fail('not_object');
  }

  if (hasForbiddenKey(input)) {
    return fail('forbidden_key');
  }

  try {
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(input);
    if (typeof serialized !== 'string') {
      return fail('unserializable');

diff --git a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..3e8837c8b61897aab24301594600f988578c3adc

+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { isNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import type { DenyReason, PrivacyPolicy } from './privacy';
+
+export type OpenNodeDeny =
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'invalid_id' }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'unknown_id'; nodeId: NodeId }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'stale_mapping'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI }
+  | {
+      allowed: false;
+      reason: 'privacy_denied';
+      nodeId: NodeId;
+      uri: URI;
+      denyReason: DenyReason;
+    };
+
+export type OpenNodeAllow = { allowed: true; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI };
+
+export type OpenNodeResult = OpenNodeAllow | OpenNodeDeny;
+
+export function resolveNodeOpen(
+  payload: unknown,
+  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI>,
+  policy: PrivacyPolicy,
+  isInWorkspace: (uri: URI) => boolean
+): OpenNodeResult {
+  if (!isNodeId(payload)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'invalid_id' };
+  }
+
+  const uri = idToUri.get(payload);
+  if (uri === undefined) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'unknown_id', nodeId: payload };
+  }
+
+  if (!isInWorkspace(uri)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'stale_mapping', nodeId: payload, uri };
+  }
+
+  if (uri.scheme !== 'file') {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const decision = policy.decide(uri.toFsPath());
+  if (!decision.allowed) {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: decision.reason as DenyReason,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return { allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri };
+}

+import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import * as fs from 'node:fs';
+import * as os from 'node:os';
+import * as path from 'node:path';
+import { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { makeNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import { resolveNodeOpen } from '../open-node';
+import { createPrivacyPolicy } from '../privacy';
+
+let fixtureRoot: string;
+let profilePath: string;
+let notePath: string;
+let policy: ReturnType<typeof createPrivacyPolicy>;
+let profileId: NodeId;
+let noteId: NodeId;
+
+beforeAll(() => {
+  fixtureRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'pa-hub-open-node-'));
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me'), { recursive: true });
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes'), { recursive: true });
+
+  profilePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me', 'profile.md');
+  notePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes', 'hello.md');
+  fs.writeFileSync(profilePath, 'profile sentinel');
+  fs.writeFileSync(notePath, 'hello');
+
+  policy = createPrivacyPolicy({ roots: [fixtureRoot], excludeGlobs: [] });
+  profileId = makeNodeId('vault', 'knowledge/me/profile.md');
+  noteId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/hello.md');
+});
+
+afterAll(() => {
+  fs.rmSync(fixtureRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
+});
+
+describe('resolveNodeOpen', () => {
+  it('test_open_node_invalid_id', () => {
+    const idToUri = new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, URI.file(notePath)]]);
+
+    for (const payload of [null, '', 'not:a:valid:id', 42]) {
+      expect(resolveNodeOpen(payload, idToUri, policy, () => true)).toEqual({
+        allowed: false,
+        reason: 'invalid_id',
+      });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_unknown_id', () => {
+    const unknownId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/missing.md');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      unknownId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>(),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'unknown_id',
+      nodeId: unknownId,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_stale_mapping', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => false
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'stale_mapping',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_mandatory', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(profilePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      profileId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[profileId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: profileId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'mandatory_exclusion',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_nonfile', () => {
+    const uri = URI.parse('notes/hello.md', 'placeholder');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_allowed', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true, nodeId: noteId, uri });
+  });
+});

codex
Both requested files are in place. The implementation follows the four gates in order, short-circuits non-file URIs to `outside_roots`, and invokes `policy.decide` only for file URIs on each call. I’m checking scope and then running the supplied acceptance gates.

 succeeded in 0ms:
import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
import { isNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
  'resolution-mode': 'import',
};
import type { DenyReason, PrivacyPolicy } from './privacy';

export type OpenNodeDeny =
  | { allowed: false; reason: 'invalid_id' }
  | { allowed: false; reason: 'unknown_id'; nodeId: NodeId }
  | { allowed: false; reason: 'stale_mapping'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI }
  | {
      allowed: false;
      reason: 'privacy_denied';
      nodeId: NodeId;
      uri: URI;
      denyReason: DenyReason;
    };

export type OpenNodeAllow = { allowed: true; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI };

export type OpenNodeResult = OpenNodeAllow | OpenNodeDeny;

export function resolveNodeOpen(
  payload: unknown,
  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI>,
  policy: PrivacyPolicy,
  isInWorkspace: (uri: URI) => boolean
): OpenNodeResult {
  if (!isNodeId(payload)) {
    return { allowed: false, reason: 'invalid_id' };
  }

  const uri = idToUri.get(payload);
  if (uri === undefined) {
    return { allowed: false, reason: 'unknown_id', nodeId: payload };
  }

  if (!isInWorkspace(uri)) {
    return { allowed: false, reason: 'stale_mapping', nodeId: payload, uri };
  }

  if (uri.scheme !== 'file') {
    return {
      allowed: false,
      reason: 'privacy_denied',
      nodeId: payload,
      uri,
      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
    };
  }

  const decision = policy.decide(uri.toFsPath());
  if (!decision.allowed) {
    return {
      allowed: false,
      reason: 'privacy_denied',
      nodeId: payload,
      uri,
      denyReason: decision.reason as DenyReason,
    };
  }

  return { allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri };
}

import * as os from 'node:os';

import { URI } from '@foam/core';

import { makeNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
  'resolution-mode': 'import',
};
import { resolveNodeOpen } from '../open-node';
import { createPrivacyPolicy } from '../privacy';

let profilePath: string;
let notePath: string;
let policy: ReturnType<typeof createPrivacyPolicy>;
let profileId: NodeId;
let noteId: NodeId;

  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me'), { recursive: true });
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes'), { recursive: true });

  profilePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me', 'profile.md');
  notePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes', 'hello.md');
  fs.writeFileSync(profilePath, 'profile sentinel');
  fs.writeFileSync(notePath, 'hello');

  policy = createPrivacyPolicy({ roots: [fixtureRoot], excludeGlobs: [] });
  profileId = makeNodeId('vault', 'knowledge/me/profile.md');
  noteId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/hello.md');

afterAll(() => {
  fs.rmSync(fixtureRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });

describe('resolveNodeOpen', () => {
  it('test_open_node_invalid_id', () => {
    const idToUri = new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, URI.file(notePath)]]);

    for (const payload of [null, '', 'not:a:valid:id', 42]) {
      expect(resolveNodeOpen(payload, idToUri, policy, () => true)).toEqual({

      });
    }
  });

  it('test_open_node_unknown_id', () => {

    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
      unknownId,
      new Map<NodeId, URI>(),
      policy,
      () => true
    );

    expect(result).toEqual({
      allowed: false,
      reason: 'unknown_id',
      nodeId: unknownId,
    });
  });

  it('test_open_node_stale_mapping', () => {
    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
      noteId,
      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
      policy,
      () => false
    );

    expect(result).toEqual({
      allowed: false,
      reason: 'stale_mapping',
      nodeId: noteId,
      uri,
    });
  });

  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_mandatory', () => {
    const uri = URI.file(profilePath);
    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
      profileId,
      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[profileId, uri]]),
      policy,
      () => true
    );

    expect(result).toEqual({
      allowed: false,
      reason: 'privacy_denied',
      nodeId: profileId,
      uri,
      denyReason: 'mandatory_exclusion',
    });
  });

  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_nonfile', () => {
    const uri = URI.parse('notes/hello.md', 'placeholder');
    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
      noteId,
      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
      policy,
      () => true
    );

    expect(result).toEqual({
      allowed: false,
      reason: 'privacy_denied',
      nodeId: noteId,
      uri,
      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
    });
  });

  it('test_open_node_allowed', () => {
    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
      noteId,
      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
      policy,
      () => true
    );

    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true, nodeId: noteId, uri });
  });
});

diff --git a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..3e8837c8b61897aab24301594600f988578c3adc
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { isNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import type { DenyReason, PrivacyPolicy } from './privacy';
+
+export type OpenNodeDeny =
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'invalid_id' }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'unknown_id'; nodeId: NodeId }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'stale_mapping'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI }
+  | {
+      allowed: false;
+      reason: 'privacy_denied';
+      nodeId: NodeId;
+      uri: URI;
+      denyReason: DenyReason;
+    };
+
+export type OpenNodeAllow = { allowed: true; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI };
+
+export type OpenNodeResult = OpenNodeAllow | OpenNodeDeny;
+
+export function resolveNodeOpen(
+  payload: unknown,
+  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI>,
+  policy: PrivacyPolicy,
+  isInWorkspace: (uri: URI) => boolean
+): OpenNodeResult {
+  if (!isNodeId(payload)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'invalid_id' };
+  }
+
+  const uri = idToUri.get(payload);
+  if (uri === undefined) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'unknown_id', nodeId: payload };
+  }
+
+  if (!isInWorkspace(uri)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'stale_mapping', nodeId: payload, uri };
+  }
+
+  if (uri.scheme !== 'file') {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const decision = policy.decide(uri.toFsPath());
+  if (!decision.allowed) {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: decision.reason as DenyReason,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return { allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri };
+}

+import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import * as fs from 'node:fs';
+import * as os from 'node:os';
+import * as path from 'node:path';
+import { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { makeNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import { resolveNodeOpen } from '../open-node';
+import { createPrivacyPolicy } from '../privacy';
+
+let fixtureRoot: string;
+let profilePath: string;
+let notePath: string;
+let policy: ReturnType<typeof createPrivacyPolicy>;
+let profileId: NodeId;
+let noteId: NodeId;
+
+beforeAll(() => {
+  fixtureRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'pa-hub-open-node-'));
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me'), { recursive: true });
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes'), { recursive: true });
+
+  profilePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me', 'profile.md');
+  notePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes', 'hello.md');
+  fs.writeFileSync(profilePath, 'profile sentinel');
+  fs.writeFileSync(notePath, 'hello');
+
+  policy = createPrivacyPolicy({ roots: [fixtureRoot], excludeGlobs: [] });
+  profileId = makeNodeId('vault', 'knowledge/me/profile.md');
+  noteId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/hello.md');
+});
+
+afterAll(() => {
+  fs.rmSync(fixtureRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
+});
+
+describe('resolveNodeOpen', () => {
+  it('test_open_node_invalid_id', () => {
+    const idToUri = new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, URI.file(notePath)]]);
+
+    for (const payload of [null, '', 'not:a:valid:id', 42]) {
+      expect(resolveNodeOpen(payload, idToUri, policy, () => true)).toEqual({
+        allowed: false,
+        reason: 'invalid_id',
+      });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_unknown_id', () => {
+    const unknownId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/missing.md');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      unknownId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>(),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'unknown_id',
+      nodeId: unknownId,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_stale_mapping', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => false
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'stale_mapping',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_mandatory', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(profilePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      profileId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[profileId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: profileId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'mandatory_exclusion',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_nonfile', () => {
+    const uri = URI.parse('notes/hello.md', 'placeholder');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_allowed', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true, nodeId: noteId, uri });
+  });
+});

+    };
+  }

+  return { allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri };
+}

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..3e8837c8b61897aab24301594600f988578c3adc

+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { isNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import type { DenyReason, PrivacyPolicy } from './privacy';
+
+export type OpenNodeDeny =
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'invalid_id' }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'unknown_id'; nodeId: NodeId }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'stale_mapping'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI }
+  | {
+      allowed: false;
+      reason: 'privacy_denied';
+      nodeId: NodeId;
+      uri: URI;
+      denyReason: DenyReason;
+    };
+
+export type OpenNodeAllow = { allowed: true; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI };
+
+export type OpenNodeResult = OpenNodeAllow | OpenNodeDeny;
+
+export function resolveNodeOpen(
+  payload: unknown,
+  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI>,
+  policy: PrivacyPolicy,
+  isInWorkspace: (uri: URI) => boolean
+): OpenNodeResult {
+  if (!isNodeId(payload)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'invalid_id' };
+  }
+
+  const uri = idToUri.get(payload);
+  if (uri === undefined) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'unknown_id', nodeId: payload };
+  }
+
+  if (!isInWorkspace(uri)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'stale_mapping', nodeId: payload, uri };
+  }
+
+  if (uri.scheme !== 'file') {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const decision = policy.decide(uri.toFsPath());
+  if (!decision.allowed) {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: decision.reason as DenyReason,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return { allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri };
+}

+import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import * as fs from 'node:fs';
+import * as os from 'node:os';
+import * as path from 'node:path';
+import { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { makeNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import { resolveNodeOpen } from '../open-node';
+import { createPrivacyPolicy } from '../privacy';
+
+let fixtureRoot: string;
+let profilePath: string;
+let notePath: string;
+let policy: ReturnType<typeof createPrivacyPolicy>;
+let profileId: NodeId;
+let noteId: NodeId;
+
+beforeAll(() => {
+  fixtureRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'pa-hub-open-node-'));
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me'), { recursive: true });
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes'), { recursive: true });
+
+  profilePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me', 'profile.md');
+  notePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes', 'hello.md');
+  fs.writeFileSync(profilePath, 'profile sentinel');
+  fs.writeFileSync(notePath, 'hello');
+
+  policy = createPrivacyPolicy({ roots: [fixtureRoot], excludeGlobs: [] });
+  profileId = makeNodeId('vault', 'knowledge/me/profile.md');
+  noteId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/hello.md');
+});
+
+afterAll(() => {
+  fs.rmSync(fixtureRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
+});
+
+describe('resolveNodeOpen', () => {
+  it('test_open_node_invalid_id', () => {
+    const idToUri = new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, URI.file(notePath)]]);
+
+    for (const payload of [null, '', 'not:a:valid:id', 42]) {
+      expect(resolveNodeOpen(payload, idToUri, policy, () => true)).toEqual({
+        allowed: false,
+        reason: 'invalid_id',
+      });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_unknown_id', () => {
+    const unknownId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/missing.md');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      unknownId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>(),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'unknown_id',
+      nodeId: unknownId,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_stale_mapping', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => false
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'stale_mapping',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_mandatory', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(profilePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      profileId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[profileId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: profileId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'mandatory_exclusion',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_nonfile', () => {
+    const uri = URI.parse('notes/hello.md', 'placeholder');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_allowed', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true, nodeId: noteId, uri });
+  });
+});

@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { isNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import type { DenyReason, PrivacyPolicy } from './privacy';
+
+export type OpenNodeDeny =
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'invalid_id' }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'unknown_id'; nodeId: NodeId }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'stale_mapping'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI }
+  | {
+      allowed: false;
+      reason: 'privacy_denied';
+      nodeId: NodeId;
+      uri: URI;
+      denyReason: DenyReason;
+    };
+
+export type OpenNodeAllow = { allowed: true; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI };
+
+export type OpenNodeResult = OpenNodeAllow | OpenNodeDeny;
+
+export function resolveNodeOpen(
+  payload: unknown,
+  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI>,
+  policy: PrivacyPolicy,
+  isInWorkspace: (uri: URI) => boolean
+): OpenNodeResult {
+  if (!isNodeId(payload)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'invalid_id' };
+  }
+
+  const uri = idToUri.get(payload);
+  if (uri === undefined) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'unknown_id', nodeId: payload };
+  }
+
+  if (!isInWorkspace(uri)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'stale_mapping', nodeId: payload, uri };
+  }
+
+  if (uri.scheme !== 'file') {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const decision = policy.decide(uri.toFsPath());
+  if (!decision.allowed) {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: decision.reason as DenyReason,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return { allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri };
+}

+import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import * as fs from 'node:fs';
+import * as os from 'node:os';
+import * as path from 'node:path';
+import { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { makeNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import { resolveNodeOpen } from '../open-node';
+import { createPrivacyPolicy } from '../privacy';
+
+let fixtureRoot: string;
+let profilePath: string;
+let notePath: string;
+let policy: ReturnType<typeof createPrivacyPolicy>;
+let profileId: NodeId;
+let noteId: NodeId;
+
+beforeAll(() => {
+  fixtureRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'pa-hub-open-node-'));
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me'), { recursive: true });
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes'), { recursive: true });
+
+  profilePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me', 'profile.md');
+  notePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes', 'hello.md');
+  fs.writeFileSync(profilePath, 'profile sentinel');
+  fs.writeFileSync(notePath, 'hello');
+
+  policy = createPrivacyPolicy({ roots: [fixtureRoot], excludeGlobs: [] });
+  profileId = makeNodeId('vault', 'knowledge/me/profile.md');
+  noteId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/hello.md');
+});
+
+afterAll(() => {
+  fs.rmSync(fixtureRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
+});
+
+describe('resolveNodeOpen', () => {
+  it('test_open_node_invalid_id', () => {
+    const idToUri = new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, URI.file(notePath)]]);
+
+    for (const payload of [null, '', 'not:a:valid:id', 42]) {
+      expect(resolveNodeOpen(payload, idToUri, policy, () => true)).toEqual({
+        allowed: false,
+        reason: 'invalid_id',
+      });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_unknown_id', () => {
+    const unknownId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/missing.md');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      unknownId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>(),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'unknown_id',
+      nodeId: unknownId,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_stale_mapping', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => false
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'stale_mapping',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_mandatory', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(profilePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      profileId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[profileId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: profileId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'mandatory_exclusion',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_nonfile', () => {
+    const uri = URI.parse('notes/hello.md', 'placeholder');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_allowed', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true, nodeId: noteId, uri });
+  });
+});

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""k_N11a_1: static check for 013a open-node domain module."""
import os, subprocess, sys

FX = "/Users/slee65/projects/lazarus-foam-ext"
MOD = os.path.join(FX, "packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts")
TST = os.path.join(FX, "packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/tests/test_open_node.test.ts")
fails = []

if not os.path.isfile(MOD):
    fails.append("open-node.ts missing")
if not os.path.isfile(TST):
    fails.append("test file missing")

if fails:
    for f in fails:
        print(f"FAIL k_N11a_1 {f}")
    sys.exit(1)

mod = open(MOD).read()
tst = open(TST).read()

# No vscode import (pure domain module)
if "from 'vscode'" in mod or 'from "vscode"' in mod or "import * as vscode" in mod:
    fails.append("open-node.ts must not import vscode")

# Must export a function
if "export function" not in mod and "export const" not in mod:
    fails.append("open-node.ts has no exports")

# Must reference PrivacyPolicy
if "PrivacyPolicy" not in mod:
    fails.append("open-node.ts missing PrivacyPolicy reference")

# Must reference NodeId
if "NodeId" not in mod:
    fails.append("open-node.ts missing NodeId reference")

# Must reference idToUri or Map<NodeId
if "idToUri" not in mod and "Map<NodeId" not in mod:
    fails.append("open-node.ts missing idToUri/Map<NodeId>")

# Must have discriminated union result with deny reasons
for reason in ["invalid_id", "unknown_id", "stale_mapping", "privacy_denied"]:
    if reason not in mod:
        fails.append(f"open-node.ts missing deny reason '{reason}'")

# Must reference isNodeId
if "isNodeId" not in mod:
    fails.append("open-node.ts missing isNodeId validation")

# Test count
test_count = tst.count("test_open_node_")
if test_count < 6:
    fails.append(f"test file has {test_count} test_open_node_ refs (need >= 6)")

# tsc
tsc = os.path.join(FX, "node_modules/.bin/tsc")
r = subprocess.run([tsc, "--noEmit", "-p", os.path.join(FX, "packages/foam-vscode/tsconfig.json")],
                    capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
tsc_errors = sum(1 for line in r.stdout.splitlines() if "error TS" in line)
if tsc_errors > 0:
    fails.append(f"tsc errors={tsc_errors}")
    for line in r.stdout.splitlines()[:5]:
        if "error TS" in line:
            print(f"  {line}")

if fails:
    for f in fails:
        print(f"FAIL k_N11a_1 {f}")
    sys.exit(1)

print(f"PASS k_N11a_1 mod=ok tst={test_count} tsc=0")

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# k_N11a_2: regression check for 013a open-node
FX="/Users/slee65/projects/lazarus-foam-ext"
PKG="$FX/packages/foam-vscode"
FAILS=0

# 1. vitest test_open_node_ tests (must be >= 6)
VJSON="/tmp/pa_hub_gate_n11a_vitest.json"
(cd "$FX" && node_modules/.bin/vitest run --root packages/foam-vscode \
  -t "test_open_node_" --reporter=json --outputFile="$VJSON" --passWithNoTests 2>&1) || true
PASS_CT=$(python3 -c "import json; d=json.load(open('$VJSON')); print(d['numPassedTests'])" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
FAIL_CT=$(python3 -c "import json; d=json.load(open('$VJSON')); print(d['numFailedTests'])" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
if [ "$PASS_CT" -lt 6 ] || [ "$FAIL_CT" -gt 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL k_N11a_2 vitest pass=$PASS_CT fail=$FAIL_CT (need >=6/0)"
  FAILS=$((FAILS+1))
fi

# 2. tsc
TSC_OUT=$("$FX/node_modules/.bin/tsc" --noEmit -p "$PKG/tsconfig.json" 2>&1 || true)
TSC_ERRORS=$(echo "$TSC_OUT" | /usr/bin/grep -cE 'error TS' || true)
if [ "$TSC_ERRORS" -gt 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL k_N11a_2 tsc errors=$TSC_ERRORS"
  echo "$TSC_OUT" | head -5
  FAILS=$((FAILS+1))
fi

# 3. oxlint
LINT_OUT=$(cd "$PKG" && ../../node_modules/.bin/oxlint src/pa-hub/open-node.ts 2>&1 || true)
LINT_ERR=$(echo "$LINT_OUT" | /usr/bin/grep -oE '[0-9]+ error' | /usr/bin/grep -oE '^[0-9]+' || echo 0)
if [ "$LINT_ERR" -gt 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL k_N11a_2 oxlint errors=$LINT_ERR"
  FAILS=$((FAILS+1))
fi

# 4. regression gates
for k in identity graph_protocol privacy_policy sidebar_manifest graph_renderer claude_sidecar claude_usage roots webview_security graph_view; do
  GATE_OUT=$(cd "$FX" && python3 scripts/pa_hub_gate.py -k "$k" 2>&1 | /usr/bin/grep '^PASS\|^FAIL' || echo "ERROR $k")
  if echo "$GATE_OUT" | /usr/bin/grep -q '^FAIL\|^ERROR'; then
    echo "FAIL k_N11a_2 regression gate $k: $GATE_OUT"
    FAILS=$((FAILS+1))
  fi
done

if [ "$FAILS" -gt 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL k_N11a_2 total_failures=$FAILS"
  exit 1
fi
echo "PASS k_N11a_2 vitest=$PASS_CT tsc=0 oxlint=$LINT_ERR regressions=ok"

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..3e8837c8b61897aab24301594600f988578c3adc
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/open-node.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { isNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import type { DenyReason, PrivacyPolicy } from './privacy';
+
+export type OpenNodeDeny =
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'invalid_id' }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'unknown_id'; nodeId: NodeId }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'stale_mapping'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI }
+  | {
+      allowed: false;
+      reason: 'privacy_denied';
+      nodeId: NodeId;
+      uri: URI;
+      denyReason: DenyReason;
+    };
+
+export type OpenNodeAllow = { allowed: true; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI };
+
+export type OpenNodeResult = OpenNodeAllow | OpenNodeDeny;
+
+export function resolveNodeOpen(
+  payload: unknown,
+  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI>,
+  policy: PrivacyPolicy,
+  isInWorkspace: (uri: URI) => boolean
+): OpenNodeResult {
+  if (!isNodeId(payload)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'invalid_id' };
+  }
+
+  const uri = idToUri.get(payload);
+  if (uri === undefined) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'unknown_id', nodeId: payload };
+  }
+
+  if (!isInWorkspace(uri)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'stale_mapping', nodeId: payload, uri };
+  }
+
+  if (uri.scheme !== 'file') {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const decision = policy.decide(uri.toFsPath());
+  if (!decision.allowed) {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: decision.reason as DenyReason,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return { allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri };
+}

+import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import * as fs from 'node:fs';
+import * as os from 'node:os';
+import * as path from 'node:path';
+import { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { makeNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import { resolveNodeOpen } from '../open-node';
+import { createPrivacyPolicy } from '../privacy';
+
+let fixtureRoot: string;
+let profilePath: string;
+let notePath: string;
+let policy: ReturnType<typeof createPrivacyPolicy>;
+let profileId: NodeId;
+let noteId: NodeId;
+
+beforeAll(() => {
+  fixtureRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'pa-hub-open-node-'));
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me'), { recursive: true });
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes'), { recursive: true });
+
+  profilePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me', 'profile.md');
+  notePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes', 'hello.md');
+  fs.writeFileSync(profilePath, 'profile sentinel');
+  fs.writeFileSync(notePath, 'hello');
+
+  policy = createPrivacyPolicy({ roots: [fixtureRoot], excludeGlobs: [] });
+  profileId = makeNodeId('vault', 'knowledge/me/profile.md');
+  noteId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/hello.md');
+});
+
+afterAll(() => {
+  fs.rmSync(fixtureRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
+});
+
+describe('resolveNodeOpen', () => {
+  it('test_open_node_invalid_id', () => {
+    const idToUri = new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, URI.file(notePath)]]);
+
+    for (const payload of [null, '', 'not:a:valid:id', 42]) {
+      expect(resolveNodeOpen(payload, idToUri, policy, () => true)).toEqual({
+        allowed: false,
+        reason: 'invalid_id',
+      });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_unknown_id', () => {
+    const unknownId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/missing.md');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      unknownId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>(),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'unknown_id',
+      nodeId: unknownId,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_stale_mapping', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => false
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'stale_mapping',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_mandatory', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(profilePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      profileId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[profileId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: profileId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'mandatory_exclusion',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_nonfile', () => {
+    const uri = URI.parse('notes/hello.md', 'placeholder');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_allowed', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true, nodeId: noteId, uri });
+  });
+});

@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { isNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import type { DenyReason, PrivacyPolicy } from './privacy';
+
+export type OpenNodeDeny =
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'invalid_id' }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'unknown_id'; nodeId: NodeId }
+  | { allowed: false; reason: 'stale_mapping'; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI }
+  | {
+      allowed: false;
+      reason: 'privacy_denied';
+      nodeId: NodeId;
+      uri: URI;
+      denyReason: DenyReason;
+    };
+
+export type OpenNodeAllow = { allowed: true; nodeId: NodeId; uri: URI };
+
+export type OpenNodeResult = OpenNodeAllow | OpenNodeDeny;
+
+export function resolveNodeOpen(
+  payload: unknown,
+  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI>,
+  policy: PrivacyPolicy,
+  isInWorkspace: (uri: URI) => boolean
+): OpenNodeResult {
+  if (!isNodeId(payload)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'invalid_id' };
+  }
+
+  const uri = idToUri.get(payload);
+  if (uri === undefined) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'unknown_id', nodeId: payload };
+  }
+
+  if (!isInWorkspace(uri)) {
+    return { allowed: false, reason: 'stale_mapping', nodeId: payload, uri };
+  }
+
+  if (uri.scheme !== 'file') {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const decision = policy.decide(uri.toFsPath());
+  if (!decision.allowed) {
+    return {
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: payload,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: decision.reason as DenyReason,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return { allowed: true, nodeId: payload, uri };
+}

+import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import * as fs from 'node:fs';
+import * as os from 'node:os';
+import * as path from 'node:path';
+import { URI } from '@foam/core';
+// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
+import { makeNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
+import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
+  'resolution-mode': 'import',
+};
+import { resolveNodeOpen } from '../open-node';
+import { createPrivacyPolicy } from '../privacy';
+
+let fixtureRoot: string;
+let profilePath: string;
+let notePath: string;
+let policy: ReturnType<typeof createPrivacyPolicy>;
+let profileId: NodeId;
+let noteId: NodeId;
+
+beforeAll(() => {
+  fixtureRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'pa-hub-open-node-'));
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me'), { recursive: true });
+  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes'), { recursive: true });
+
+  profilePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'knowledge', 'me', 'profile.md');
+  notePath = path.join(fixtureRoot, 'notes', 'hello.md');
+  fs.writeFileSync(profilePath, 'profile sentinel');
+  fs.writeFileSync(notePath, 'hello');
+
+  policy = createPrivacyPolicy({ roots: [fixtureRoot], excludeGlobs: [] });
+  profileId = makeNodeId('vault', 'knowledge/me/profile.md');
+  noteId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/hello.md');
+});
+
+afterAll(() => {
+  fs.rmSync(fixtureRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
+});
+
+describe('resolveNodeOpen', () => {
+  it('test_open_node_invalid_id', () => {
+    const idToUri = new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, URI.file(notePath)]]);
+
+    for (const payload of [null, '', 'not:a:valid:id', 42]) {
+      expect(resolveNodeOpen(payload, idToUri, policy, () => true)).toEqual({
+        allowed: false,
+        reason: 'invalid_id',
+      });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_unknown_id', () => {
+    const unknownId = makeNodeId('vault', 'notes/missing.md');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      unknownId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>(),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'unknown_id',
+      nodeId: unknownId,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_stale_mapping', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => false
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'stale_mapping',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_mandatory', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(profilePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      profileId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[profileId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: profileId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'mandatory_exclusion',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_privacy_denied_nonfile', () => {
+    const uri = URI.parse('notes/hello.md', 'placeholder');
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      allowed: false,
+      reason: 'privacy_denied',
+      nodeId: noteId,
+      uri,
+      denyReason: 'outside_roots',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('test_open_node_allowed', () => {
+    const uri = URI.file(notePath);
+    const result = resolveNodeOpen(
+      noteId,
+      new Map<NodeId, URI>([[noteId, uri]]),
+      policy,
+      () => true
+    );
+
+    expect(result).toEqual({ allowed: true, nodeId: noteId, uri });
+  });
+});
